# CMP_LT fork (WIP) — Milestone P2 relational ordering

Status: **NOT_DEMONSTRATED** (8/12 on the harness; the VM core is intact; the
compare automaton reaches its verdict but the classification exit never hands
control to the boolean push blocks, so `OUT` re-pops Slot 1 and prints the
operand `b` — findings 14-20. The "init-basin lottery" framing of findings
7-12 is **superseded**: the defect is deterministic (finding 17)).

## What exists

| item | evidence |
|---|---|
| Forked HeLL source | `vm_malbolge/src/mbir_cmp_lt.hell`, generated by `vm_malbolge/tools/mbir_cmp_lt_gen.py` (a fork of `mbir_p2_cmp_gen.py`; the frozen CMP_EQ image is untouched) |
| Assembled image | `vm_malbolge/src/mbir_cmp_lt.mb` (57,518 bytes < 59,049 limit, 1,531 bytes headroom) + `vm_malbolge/src/mbir_cmp_lt.dbg` (the generator calls LMAO with `-d`, so `.mb` and `.dbg` always come from the same layout — two separate `-d` builds of the same source differ in padding bytes, never trace against a stale `.dbg`) |
| Differential harness | 9 ordered pairs over domain {0,1,2} plus 3 baselines, run on `tools/oracle_classic.py` (as a module, with stdin) and `runners/malbolge-original/malbolge` |

## What IS demonstrated

| item | evidence |
|---|---|
| Image builds within the memory budget | LMAO 0.6.0 normal mode, 57,518 bytes (committed HEAD build), 1,531 bytes headroom, zero new flags (the marker rides on `SLOT1_FLAG`, cleared at the pass-1 entry) |
| VM core intact on the fork | Baselines 1-3 pass on BOTH engines with identical steps (re-verified 2026-10-11 on the committed HEAD image): `01 41 10 00` → `41` @67,563; `01 41 01 42 10 10 00` → `42 41` @74,600; `00` → empty @60,520 |
| Compare flow reaches its decision | Oracle step trace: `dispatch_02 → reset → add_val_clean (pass 1, pop b) → dec#1 → lt_dec2 → dec#2 → lt_dec2_ret → lt_b_ge2 → reset → lt_a_pop (pass 2, pop a) → lt_p2_dec1 → dec#1 → lt_a_dec1_ret → lt_dec2 → dec#2 → lt_dec2_ret → lt_a_dec2_ret → lt_true/lt_false → diff_is_2 → result push → add_push0_done` for every b-class branch |


## What is NOT demonstrated

| item | evidence |
|---|---|
| `CMP_LT` on the 9 ordered pairs | **8/12 pass** (5 of the 6 false-valued pairs — `(2,2)` fails — plus all 3 baselines, with perfect oracle/runner parity on every vector including the failures). The 4 remaining failures share one root cause, refined below. |
| Observed failure matrix | `(0,1)->00` (want `01`), `(0,2)->02`, `(1,2)->02` (want `01`), `(2,2)->02` (want `00`). Read as a function of `b` alone: `OUT` prints `00` for `b in {0,1}` and `02` for `b==2`, **independent of `a`** — see finding 14. |
| `2 < 4` and any value >= 3 | Out of the claimed domain {0,1,2} by design. |

## Root cause (isolated, one line from fixed) — **[HISTORICAL: superseded by findings 14-20]**

The decrement tail's `SUBROUTINE_FLAG6` line is pointed at `lt_false` (a leftover
from an earlier revision). The b-class `dec#2` is armed with `SUBROUTINE_FLAG6`,
so its return lands on that line and pushes the boolean immediately, skipping
the pass-2 transition entirely. Traced and confirmed: after `lt_dec2`'s
`dec#2`, the tail's SUB6 line fires and control reaches `lt_false` at step
~70,952 without any `reset_and_loop` between. The a-class `dec#2` (also armed
SUB6 by the shared `lt_dec2`) returns to the same place, which is why every
`b>=1` vector skips pass 2.

**Why the obvious fix does not land:** repointing that line to `lt_dec2_ret`
(the shared dispatcher that already separates pass 1 from pass 2 via
`PUSH_FLAG4`) is semantically correct, but it changes an ADDRESS VALUE in the
compiled image. LMAO's init-code generation is value-dependent (the
data-module crazy chain for a constant has a value-dependent length), so the
init code grows, the whole program re-lays-out, and the xlat2 cycle of the
`SLOT1_FLAG` trampoline that the post-push OUT depends on shifts: the OUT then
pops the cleared Slot 1 instead of Slot 0 and prints `a8` in an EOF loop.
Confirmed experimentally (v19): the repoint breaks all 9 vectors, including
`(0,0)` which was passing.

## Established facts for the next attempt

1. **Single-pass is impossible**: exhaustive search over read orders x op
   sequences (crz with C0/C1/C2/C20/C21, dec) x 3-dec signatures found zero
   programs separating `a<b`, because `(0,0)` and `(0,1)` are
   informationally identical after the standard pop idiom (both give
   value=0, value_C1=29524).
2. **The two-pass design with a marker is necessary and works** up to the
   routing bug above.
3. **The marker must be a virgin gate.** `SLOT1_FLAG` is consumed by the
   post-push OUT path; extra executions on it (dummy clears, arms, checks)
   shift its xlat2 cycle and the OUT starts popping Slot 1. The dedicated
   `LT_B1` flag (added at 2 cells) fixes this and is what the current
   revision uses.
4. **The `OP1_FLAG` line at the head of `return_from_dec_op2_1` is
   load-bearing**: removing it makes the b-class `dec#1` take the `lt_dec2`
   branch even when `b==0` (traced: the NO_MORE_CARRY check fires after
   `dec(0)`), which loops forever.
5. **NO_MORE_CARRY polarity confirmed on EQ**: armed iff the pre-decrement
   value was >= 1 (no borrow). EQ(2,0) falls through `NO_MORE_CARRY_FLAG
   op2_nonzero` straight to `diff_is_2`.
6. **The memory budget is knife-edged**: the compare section may not exceed
   roughly +10 atoms over EQ's (130); 57,517 bytes with 1,532 headroom is the
   current best, and a single reference-value change can push the init code
   over.
7. **Decisive negative result (measured 3 builds per variant)**: the routing
   fix cannot be a repoint. Repointing the tail's `SUBROUTINE_FLAG6` line to
   the dispatcher (`lt_dec2_ret`) - semantically correct, ZERO atom change -
   breaks EVERY CMP vector including `(0,0)`, which never executes that line:
   all nine print `a8` in an EOF loop, and the LIFO baseline's second OUT
   stops popping Slot 0 (`42` instead of `4241`). Repeating the build three
   times gives the same 0/9, so it is not layout lottery. Cause: LMAO's init
   code is **byte-determinative**: the data-module constant chain that writes
   a cell's initial value has a value-dependent length, and the trampoline
   cells' effective opcodes are position-dependent (`(mem[c]+c) % 94`), so
   changing one referenced address changes the init bytes, the trampoline
   xlat2 cycles shift, and `SLOT1_FLAG` - which decides slot0-vs-slot1 in
   every OUT - flips. Adding the router line inside `lt_false` instead fails
   the build outright (init-code overlap).
8. **`.OFFSET` pinning was tried and fails closed** (measured): pinning
   `SLOT0_FLAG`/`SLOT1_FLAG` at their current addresses (57588/57592) makes the
   build fail with "Memory usage conflict ... a .OFFSET directive may
   conflict" - the pinned program blocks sit where the (now longer) init code
   needs to reach, with or without the repoint. The program region is densely
   packed (HALT 57435, OUT 57459, IN 57477, MOVD 57494, ROT 57497, NOP 57535,
   SLOT0 57588, SLOT1 57592, ...), so there is no free gap to pin into.
9. **It is not a layout shift after all** (measured, v25): the repointed
   variant produces the SAME image size (57,517), the SAME trampoline
   addresses (SLOT0 57588, SLOT1 57592, HALT 57435, OUT 57459) as the
   working 8/12 variant. Only the init-code bytes inside LMAO's constant
   generation differ (one cell's address value), yet the OUT's slot choice
   flips for every vector including `(0,0)`, which never executes the
   repointed line. Dropping the now-dead `OP1_FLAG` line at
   `return_from_dec_op2_1` (-3 atoms, correct once both `dec#2` returns route
   via the SUB6 line) does not help either. The breakage lives inside LMAO's
   init-code generation, not in anything the HeLL source controls.
10. **Both `dec#2`-via-`SUB5` variants hit a second, different bug** (v26,
    v27, traced): routing both `dec#2` returns through
    `return_from_dec_op2_1` (where the existing `OP1_FLAG` line routes them
    correctly - verified in the trace at step ~71,042) makes the b-class
    `dec#1` take the `lt_dec2` branch even when `b == 0`: the
    `NO_MORE_CARRY_FLAG` check fires after `dec(0)`, which it must not (the
    polarity is confirmed on EQ: armed iff the pre-decrement value was >= 1).
    Splitting `lt_dec2` per class costs +6 atoms and does not fit the memory
    budget (build fails). Combined with finding 9, the practical conclusion
    is that **the routing cannot be moved inside this image at all**: any
    reference/value change in the compare section lands in a different LMAO
    init-code basin where either the OUT's slot choice or the
    `NO_MORE_CARRY` semantics flips.
11. **The byte-level mechanism is pinned down**: comparing a working and a
    broken image of the same length shows the differing bytes are all in
    57416-57512 - the tail of LMAO's init code, from which the interpreter's
    crazy-fill derives the initial values of every cell after the file's end,
    including the flag trampolines (`SLOT0_FLAG` 57588, `SLOT1_FLAG` 57592).
    Trampoline opcodes are position-and-value dependent, so a different init
    tail changes the trampolines' xlat2 cycles. 14 semantically-neutral
    dead-block permutations of the correct-routing source all gave 0/9, so
    the basin is stable per compare-section content, not per layout noise.
12. **The lottery-immune fix (measured, ready to implement)**: in a broken
    basin every OUT pops Slot 1 - probed on the 0/9 image, all OUTs print
    `59048` (the EOF/fill value of the cleared Slot 1) regardless of operand,
    i.e. the slot choice is not data-dependent, it is purely the trampoline's
    xlat2 cycle. Therefore the fix that does not depend on the basin at all:
    **leave the compare result in BOTH slots.** The result push ends in
    `add_push0_done`, which is exclusive to the compare path (the OUT-opcode
    pop never passes through it - it goes `return_from_st1_4` ->
    `ret_rst_st1_3` -> `reset_and_loop` directly), and its chain currently
    RESETS Slot 1 to C1 (empty). Replacing that reset with a copy of Slot 0
    (read Slot 0 with the double-`ROT C2 R_ROT` idiom into A, crazy it into
    `stack_top_1`) costs roughly +8-10 atoms and makes the OUT print the
    result whether it pops Slot 0 (working basin) or Slot 1 (broken basin).
    Combined with the routing fix, this lands all 9 pairs.
13. **Viable next moves**: (a) implement 12 (the copy in `add_push0_done`)
    together with the `SUBROUTINE_FLAG6` repoint, accepting the atom cost and
    cutting the ~10 atoms back from the EQ-verbatim push tail (`ROT C1 R_ROT`
    in `diff_is_2` is the first candidate - it only pre-loads A for the
    `stack_scratch` crazy); (b) shrink the compare section by ~10 atoms and
    retry the routing fix alone inside that smaller basin; (c) inspect
    `third_party/lmao/src/gen_init.c` for a deterministic mode; (d) accept
    8/12 and fix the independent push-encoding defect (the true-push prints
    `02` instead of `01`).

## Findings added 2026-10-10 (new experiments, oracle==runner on every vector)

Two further routing variants were built and run against the 12-vector harness.
Both **fit** the memory budget and both reproduce `print == b`-derived value
independent of `a` (7/12), which **falsifies the "basin / slot lottery" framing**
of findings 7-12 as the primary blocker.

14. **The blocker is result propagation, not the OUT slot lottery** (measured):
    - `BASEC` = HEAD + `SUBROUTINE_FLAG6` repoint (H6) + dead-`OP1_FLAG` cut
      (OP1DEAD): builds at **56,947 bytes**, harness **7/12**, oracle==runner.
      Output is `00` for `b in {0,1}` and `02` for `b==2`, independent of `a`.
    - `13(a)` = H6 routing fix **+** the both-slots copy chain (finding 12:
      replace the `add_push0_done` Slot-1 reset with a Slot0->Slot1 copy via
      `V10ROW/V10S1/V10S2/V10S3`). Builds at **57,517 bytes** (fits), harness
      **7/12**, oracle==runner. The step trace (`lt_trace2.py` on `(0,2)`) shows
      pass 2 now **completes**: `lt_a_pop -> lt_a_dec1_ret -> lt_a_dec2_ret ->
      diff_is_2 -> add_rst0_done -> load_diff_1 -> add_push_common ->
      add_push0_done -> return_from_stack_top_3` (steps ~26.9k-30.5k), i.e. the
      compare reaches the boolean and the push automaton. **Yet `OUT` still
      prints the `b`-derived value.** With the result present in BOTH slots
      (the lottery-immune construction) the printed value is still wrong, so
      making `OUT`'s slot choice irrelevant does **not** fix it.

    Conclusion: the defect is upstream of the slot choice — the CMP result is
    never landed into a cell whose value dominates the `OUT`'s printed `A`;
    the printed value is governed by the popped operand `b` (pass-1 leftover),
    not by the pass-2 boolean. The next attempt must target the value/encoding
    of the result push and its interaction with the post-compare `OUT`
    automaton (finding 13d), not the trampoline basin.
    Artifacts (this session): `13(a)` `.mb` sha256
    `a2e8cad66ba955e567cc685cdcd0c137f01ca294aeabccd184c394a0618626d4`
    (57,517 bytes), retained outside the repo for reference. Debug tooling
    added: `vm_malbolge/tests/lt_harness.py` (12-vector differential harness)
    and `vm_malbolge/tools/lt_trace2.py` (label/op/MOVD/JMP control-flow
    tracer over the `.dbg`).

15. **Exact functional defect isolated (traced `13(a)` on `(0,2)`)**: the only
    cell that ever executes opcode `OUT` (op 5) and prints is at step **73653**
    with `A = 2` (= the operand `b`). Every other `JMP-> OUT @ 57476` is the
    crazy-fill sweep passing over the OUT cell while it is not yet in its OUT
    xlat2 phase (no print). Reading the slot cells at that step: `stack_top`
    (Slot 0) = `0` (the operand `a`) and `stack_top_1` (Slot 1) = `2` (the
    operand `b`). The two slots still hold the **original operands** — the
    compare result `1` (correct, `lt_true` does fire at steps 73624/73642) is
    never landed into Slot 0, and Slot 1 is never reset to empty. Because Slot
    1 is non-empty, the `OUT` opcode takes the `return_from_st1_4` pop-Slot-1
    branch and prints `b`.

    Contrast with the frozen, working `CMP_EQ` (`P2_STATUS.md`): after its
    compare the result is in Slot 0 and Slot 1 is cleared, so `OUT` falls to
    pop-Slot-0 and prints the boolean. **The `CMP_LT` fork reaches the correct
    verdict but does not reproduce that push/clear tail** — its push machinery
    runs (`add_push_common`/`add_push0_done`/`ret_rst_st1_3` all execute, and
    `lt_true` fires spuriously *during the push* at 73624/73642, indicating the
    compare branch and the push automaton share routing state that should be
    exclusive). This is a concrete, localizable target, not a lottery.

16. **`gen_init.c` inspected (finding 13c) — no deterministic mode**: the init
    tail length is inherently value-dependent by construction —
    `load_constant_to_a_reg(..., constant, ..., rotations, crazy_C2_first, ...)`
    emits a different instruction sequence per constant/address, and
    `set_dreg` walks D to each target. There is no flag to make the codegen
    basin-invariant; only patching vendored LMAO (out of scope, breaks
    `third_party/PROVENANCE.md`) could change that. So finding 13c is a dead
    end and finding 15 (fix the push/clear tail to match `CMP_EQ`) is the live
    path. It must be re-verified for both the 12-vector result AND the memory
    budget after every edit, because the same edits that fix the tail can
    re-roll the basin (findings 7-11).

## Findings added 2026-10-11 (deterministic-defect evidence, oracle==runner on every vector)

HEAD re-verified after restoring the tree from probe builds: harness **8/12**,
`mbir_cmp_lt.mb` sha256 `4a38722a…` (table below), failures `(0,1)->00` @79,167,
`(0,2)->02`, `(1,2)->02`, `(2,2)->02` @78,974 — identical on both engines.

17. **The present defect is deterministic, not a basin** (measured): LMAO seeds
    `srand(time(NULL))` (`third_party/lmao/src/main.c:222`) and fills padding
    with `nop_opcodes[rand()%8]` (`initialize.c:414-419`) — same source,
    different image *bytes* per build, same length and same behavior. Three
    consecutive rebuilds of `BASEC` and of `13(a)` scored the same 7/12 with
    per-vector step counts identical, so the failures of findings 14-16 are
    semantic, not layout lottery. This formally retires the "init-basin
    lottery" framing of findings 7-13 for the current blocker.

18. **Measured invariant at `OUT`** across HEAD / BASE / BASEC / 13(a): the
    printed value is operand-derived (exactly `b` in the BASE variants), and
    the two slots still hold the original operands `(a, b)`; `stack_top`
    receives no write after the operand push (BASE `(0,2)`: last
    `CRAZY->stack_top` @60,228 writes the pushed `a=0`; nothing until `HALT`
    @76,606). Slot 1 only ever sees read-idiom write-backs (1→2 pairs).

19. **`R_MOVED` restoration experiments** (BASE variants): restoring EQ's
    preserved `R_MOVED` in `diff_is_2` only (MV2) is **neutral** (7/12, same
    failing vectors); additionally restoring it in `dispatch_02` (MV12) is
    **catastrophic** (3/12: `b=0` vectors print `a8` in an EOF loop, `b>=1`
    vectors do not terminate by 600,000 steps). LT's deliberate drops of
    those `R_MOVED` lines were required by LT's different MOVED-flag state
    at dispatch.

20. **MOVd-target-level trace localizes the break** (BASE build, `(0,2)`,
    `lt_trace2.py`): pass-1 pop completes (`add_pop1_done` @69,020); the
    classify chain is genuinely entered (`return_from_dec_op2_1` @69,577;
    `lt_dec2`/`lt_dec2_ret` armed @70,113/70,118); control then lands on the
    `lt_false` head cell **six times** (70,151-70,279) — yet the
    boolean-encoder blocks never execute (no `MOVD`-arm into
    `diff_is_2`/`lt_true` targets, no `stack_scratch` crazy in that window).
    What follows instead is a ~2,900-step generic subroutine unwind
    (`return_from_decrement_value_1..3` → `disc_1`/`disc_2`, value-division
    loops 3159→1053→351→117→39→13), and the trailing generic dispatch
    performs a second Slot-1 read (`return_from_stack_top_2/3` chain,
    `return_from_st1_3` armed @73,071) so `OUT` @73,078 prints `b`.
    **Conclusion:** the verdict automaton reaches the true/false routing but
    its exit trampoline never hands control to `diff_is_2` → result push;
    the fix target is the dec-exit → classification-completion landing —
    consistent with finding 15's push/clear-tail target, and now demonstrably
    upstream of both the OUT slot choice (finding 14) and any basin concern
    (finding 17).

**Trace-methodology caveat:** the `.dbg LABELS`-range attribution in
`lt_trace2.py` misnames cells of the NOP padding sweep adjacent to a label
(e.g. the `~lt_false`/`~add_push0_done` floods in `lt_slotprobe.py` bisect
output). The trustworthy signals are `MOVd` target cells, jumps landing
exactly on label head cells, and slot/cell value-write events.

## Reproduce

```
python3 vm_malbolge/tools/mbir_cmp_lt_gen.py          # writes .hell, .mb, .dbg
printf '\x01\x01\x01\x02\x02\x10\x00' | ./runners/malbolge-original/malbolge vm_malbolge/src/mbir_cmp_lt.mb | xxd
```

## 2026-10-11 (session 2) — retarget/reorder/slot-swap battery: all size-neutral fixes falsified

Scope: `vm_malbolge/src/mbir_cmp_lt.*` rebuilt from the a7a040e generator; every
probe restores from it. Native runner sweeps (`runners/malbolge-original/malbolge`,
subprocess raw-byte stdin) plus one full dual-engine harness run.

**Finding 21 — attribution correction.** Commit `a28b894` (current HEAD) holds the
V16 EQ-verbatim probe image, not the LT design. Its LT-harness grid is `01` only at
`(2,2)` (the EQ transplant ceiling) and passes 8/12 only by coincidence. The LT
8/12 artifact is the `a7a040e` lineage. Rebuilt today from generator sha256
`bb663dcf773528afc310f42153da4493dc5ba279ba5b684dadc6045cda4703ea`
(= `git show a7a040e:vm_malbolge/tools/mbir_cmp_lt_gen.py`): 57,517 cells; harness
`8/12` with perfect oracle/runner parity, failures `(0,1)->00`,
`(0,2)->02`, `(1,2)->02`, `(2,2)->02`, steps 79167/79167/78974 per row class.

**Finding 22 — committed-hash chain is stale.** Today's rebuild produced
`.hell 4839447fb38a45be75f2c0c871ef182672404386736ce531d2eae25d6544eea5` and
`.dbg d21bd14d60e3cc1cd5bb2335defe485b76bf74788cf87c58373c359a9da9cec9` —
byte-identical to the `a7a040e` blobs — but `.mb 98c0b436ca8fda837d3e6964f75623587ad753239180391192f6992fa2e790e9`,
which differs from the `.mb` committed at `a7a040e`
(`df3fbfc4b358a16529dc7539f719850b9675b9ddf1665f29425b3bdc98f7bf52`). The previous
"Artifact hashes" table (`cb3f59e4…`/`4a38722ab…` "re-verified on disk") matches
neither `a7a040e` nor `a28b894` blobs; it described an intermediate tree state and
is superseded by this section. The harness-published 8/12 grid corresponds to the
`.mb` sha `98c0b436…`.

**Finding 23 — tail reorder is not a fix.** Four size-preserving tail variants
repoint `SUBROUTINE_FLAG6 lt_false` -> `lt_dec2_ret` AND move that line off the
last position (orders 6,1,2,3,4,5 / 1,2,3,6,5,4 / 1,2,3,4,6,5 / 6,5,4,3,2,1;
variant scripts reproducible from the edits in `mk_b24` at hashes
`ede4b56c…`,`0361f6bb…`,`4a52cd98…`,`44f98ddc…`): builds 57,511 x3 and 57,517;
ALL produce `a8a80a` on every one of the 9 pairs (baselines pass). The
"residual-D at the tail's last line poisons the EOF-drain trampolines" model
explains B1/v19 but reordering cannot exploit it: any tail whose last line is
not the original `lt_false` target derails the drain, whatever label it carries.

**Finding 24 — dispatcher-line inserts bust the budget.** Retargeting SUB6 to
`lt_b_ge2` and appending an `OP1_FLAG lt_a_dec2_ret R_OP1_FLAG` dispatcher line
into `lt_b_ge2` (C1), or also redirecting `return_from_dec_op2_1`'s OP1 target to
`lt_a_dec1_ret` (C1b): both "Memory usage conflict while generating
initialization code".

**Finding 25 — the transposed dispatcher builds but pass 2 never runs.** C1c
(swap bodies of `lt_b_ge2` and `lt_dec2_ret` so the dispatcher fits with zero net
line change, SUB6 -> `lt_b_ge2`) built at 57,517 and still gave `a8a80a` on all
9 pairs. Trace of `(0,2)`: zero runtime jumps into any of
`lt_p2_dec1 / lt_a_dec1_ret / lt_a_dec2_ret / lt_true / lt_false / diff_is_2`;
the first (and repeatedly only) entry into the LT region is the FLAG9 drain
trampoline jumping to D = the tail's residual label (address 57681 in that
layout), which then spins RESET/dispatch forever. Confirms finding 20 from the
other side: the verdict automaton is unreachable while the drain entry point is
the tail's residual — only a verdict-terminator target is drain-safe, and that
same slot is the one the routing fix needs to repurpose.

**Finding 26 — slot swaps are layout-hostile.** Changing which subroutine cell
`lt_dec2` arms (`R_SUBROUTINE_FLAG6` -> 4/1/2/3, identical token length, D5
series, script hashes `1bf12a6c…`,`e4723e39…`,`86c3d168…`,`1915b6ae…`): all fail
with "HeLL program overlaps with generated initialization code". HeLL's
init-code placement is sensitive to flag-reference context, not only program
size; "size-neutral" edits that change which flag cells a block touches are not
available on this build.

**Finding 27 — synthesis.** The routing fix (b-context dec2 return must continue
into pass 2 instead of landing on `lt_false`) requires a new dispatcher block
plus completion of the verdict push (finding 20), and every containment route
found today is blocked: insertions bust the 59,049 init budget (24, and prior
b12/b17), tail edits of any kind are drain-fatal (23, 25), and flag-cell
rebinding is layout-fatal (26). **12/12 NOT_DEMONSTRATED remains.** Next step
is not a patch of `a7a040e`: either a fresh minimal two-pass LT design that
does not inherit the EQ drain/residual structure, or a HeLL-level budget
technique (e.g. explicit `.OFFSET` layout control) before further probing.

## 2026-10-11 (session 3) — P0/P1 mechanism probes

**Finding 28 — LMAO non-determinism (correction to finding 22).** Rebuilding the
same committed `.hell` (sha `4839447f…`) produces a different `.mb` each run
(observed `98c0b436`, `b73ccd25`, `5e494eab`, `ec3a2ba2`). The generated
CRC-encoded region varies per assembler run; decoded grid and behaviour are
identical. Canonical evidence is therefore `.hell` sha + grid snapshot, not the
`.mb` sha. The df3fbfc4≠rebuild discrepancy in finding 22 was this, not a stale file.

**Finding 29 — P0 rerun reinterpretation.** Forcing the `return_from_dec_op2_1`
landing `MOVED lt_false` -> `MOVED lt_true` (variant `p0_57517.mb`, sha not
stable per finding 28) leaves every vector's output unchanged. Earlier read
("the TRUE push does not exist") is withdrawn as unsupported: the edited line
lies in a region that never executes (finding 30), so the probe was a no-op by
construction. Counter-evidence for push feasibility: baseline `01411000` prints
`41` through the same generic OUT chain, so OUT can emit arbitrary bytes; what
is missing is the verdict *computation*, not a TRUE push mechanism.

**Finding 30 — P1: the verdict chain is 100% dead on every vector.** Block-level
traces of all 12 vectors (`a7base.mb`, grid-verified 8/12 by control sweep;

table: **zero** jumps land on the head cells of `dispatch_01` `dispatch_02`
`add_val_clean` `return_from_dec_op2_1` `lt_dec2` `lt_dec2_ret` `lt_p2_dec1`
`lt_true` `lt_false` `diff_is_2` `load_diff_0` `decrement_value`. Naive
range-attribution "landings" inside `dispatch_01`/`lt_b_ge2` were helper-label
exact hits (`RESET_FLAG1`@57682, `RESET_FLAG2`@57686, `RESET_FLAG3`@57776,
`PUSH_FLAG2`@57780, `PUSH_FLAG3`@57870) or padding (`lt_a_pop`+10@57869,
57681 residual). Live traffic: the generic decrement machinery (jump table
`MOVED`/`IN`/`ROT`/`CRAZY`/flag cells ~57458-57497), the `exit_decrement_loop`
tail table (which repeatedly jumps to 29443 — a mid-grid ROT region, the drain
loop), and `value`-cell push/read. Excision margin: 111 dead labels ≈ 658 cells
— too small for the insertions falsified in findings 24/13, so excision is not
the budget lever.

**Finding 31 — live print path mapped.** OUT is reached via table cells reading
targets (`…`@58151/58152 jump into the `OUT` head cell 57458); the digit echoed
is the content of cell `value`@57640 (vector (x,2) leaves 2 there -> prints
`02`). Therefore `01` is reachable purely through live cells; the minimal-LT
problem reduces to computing `(a<b)` into `value`/slot chain before the push,
using only size-neutral relabel edits of live jump-table lines (the b24-class
edits that build). Scope of next battery: live cells only
(`{57458..57497, 57607..57686, 57776..57780, 57869..57874, 58151..58152,
58411..58438}` and their source lines).

**State:** 12/12 remains `NOT_DEMONSTRATED`. Scripts/traces retained outside

Excision choice = layout lottery; scores observed: a7 5/9, g1(E3C) 5/9,
e7v 4/9, r1 (e7v + rdo2_1 terminal retarget to lt_true) 4/9, all other

Next: deterministic wiring of a two-slot comparison (a<b as borrow chain on

### Session 4b — opcode classification decoded; CMP_LT entry wired

**Finding 36 — `dispatch_02` was a no-op stub.** Byte classification (source

B==1 -> `dispatch_01` (PUSH imm), B==2 -> `dispatch_02` (CMP_LT entry),
B>=3 -> `dispatch_fallback` (pop+OUT echo). The `a7a040e` generator wired
`dispatch_02: R_CLEAN_FLAG / MOVED reset_and_loop` — CMP_LT was NEVER
dispatched into the verdict chain in this lineage; all harness outputs were
fallback echoes of the popped slot top. This also explains the 7/12 of `e7v`
(echo of b) as the natural fallback behavior.

**Finding 37 — entry wiring executes the chain.** Retargeting the stub
(`MOVED reset_and_loop` -> `MOVED add_val_clean`; size-identical token swap

(p3c): all build and run; traces of p3b confirm `dispatch_02`,

Score unchanged (5/9): the dec2 phase then spins on a

The remaining problem is precisely: make the borrow-chain returns
(`return_from_dec_op2_1` / `lt_a_dec1_ret` / `lt_a_dec2_ret` table cells)

### Session 4c — EQ-TRUE artifact confirmed; HeLL dispatch blocks are decorative

**Finding 38 — the EQ probe's `01` is a classification-decrement artifact, not
a TRUE push.** Traces of `a28b894` src (frozen as `~/tmp_lt/eqprobe.mb/.dbg`,
`.hell` = `~/tmp_lt/eq.hell`, its dispatch_02 is also a stub): vec `22`
prints its only byte at step 77646 (`OUT A=1`) reached through `CRAZY jmp
A=1 CRAZY:70 stack_top:1` (cell 57664), and `stack_top` was last written by
the opcode classification `MOVD->d stack_top (d=57664)` events at steps
11426-12120. vec `01` never performs any `stack_top:1` CRAZY read (0
occurrences) and prints `OUT A=0`. So "01 iff (2,2)" = echo of b mutated by
decrements; no lineage (a7, EQ probe, e*, p3*) has ever executed a working
TRUE push. Ground truth for verdict push does not exist and must be created.

**Finding 39 — correction to Finding 37: the verdict chain still never
executes.** In `p3c` (e7v base, dispatch_02 body -> `MOVED lt_a_pop`) traced
on vec `12` (`tr_p3c_12.txt`, sha 556ab796...) and on baseline `b1`
(`tr_p3c_b1.txt`, sha 165df924...): `JMP-> dispatch_02 @` and
`JMP-> dispatch_01 @` occur **zero** times on every vector tested — including
`b1` which correctly prints `41`. The apparent `JMP-> add_val_clean @ 57681`
/ `JMP-> lt_dec2_ret @ 57681` "entries" are pass-throughs of the residual
drain loop: FLAG1..FLAG9 jump-table chain (cells 58527-58814) lands on cell
57681, whose runtime content is a `RESET_FLAG1`-style line that
`MOVD->d reset_and_loop (d=58070)` and loops; the label name comes from
HeLL-range resolution, not from executing that block.

**Finding 40 — consequence: HeLL control flow is decorative in this lineage.**
Runtime semantics (push, echo, halt) live in the MOVD-driven jump-table/init
region inherited from the ADD template; the `.hell` blocks only *supply cells
whose contents the tables read* (e.g. `? movd dispatch_02:29524`,
`MOVD->d lt_false (d=57880)` are operand reads, not executions). This is why
every patch acts as a layout lottery: it perturbs table operands, never
control flow. P3-on-a7 is therefore closed; 12/12 requires a fresh minimal
design (or direct init-table authoring), not further a7 excisions.

Evidence SHAs: `tr_p3c_12.txt` 556ab79680f90b7a3ee361d961d7425748bfb8c3589f104d7daca474e6325231,
`tr_p3c_b1.txt` 165df92444c84fcdfdd7cdcd817bc59793085adbc4b1d579995ec1132e7e769d,
`tr_eq_01.txt` 3a66fc0db4877a1aa90f45682077e075874b79ad15cb1c264bae86e15c299393,
`tr_eq_22.txt` 7bcde598b90620fad1ba6e92ce2ae4039d8ec10772c59848c3f44a3f154b5b81,
`p3c.mb` fd09968418ae792d12bdea1ec21a46edf38b2b558a487415c8328ec3fc6a51ab,
`p3c.dbg` 96a34903c3da71905c21d10cddfbe04ce1e91af26e215530838ada5b36c3f263.
12/12 remains `NOT_DEMONSTRATED`.

**Finding 41 — EQ ground truth: the boolean rides on jump-table PASS COUNTS,
not on block branches (independent confirmation of finding 40 in the working
lineage).** Rebuilt the canonical CMP_EQ source (`src/mbir_p2_cmp.hell`,
committed since `9fd3979`, untouched by the LT fork) with `lmao -d` as
`~/tmp_lt/eqk.{mb,dbg}` and traced `(2,2)` TRUE, `(0,1)`, `(1,0)` FALSE
(`tr_eqk_{v22,v01,v10}.txt`). Zero jumps execute any HeLL verdict block in
the WORKING EQ image too (`diff_is_0/1`, `op2_*`, `add_rst0_done`,
`check_diff_1_or_0`, `load_diff_0`: 0 occurrences). The ONLY per-vector
control-flow difference found: passes through cell **57681** resolving to
`diff_is_2_from_phase2`: 54 on `(2,2)` vs 48 on both FALSE vectors; the 6
extra passes cluster in the compare phase (steps 77,764-77,935). So in the
demonstrated kernel the verdict is a *cycle-count in the table basin around
57681*, and the digit (1/0) is selected downstream from that count (the
LOOP5/LOOP2_3 exit counters). This retires finding 15's "reproduce the push/
clear tail (by block edits)" framing: blocks cannot be reproduced to fix it
because blocks are not what runs. The viable design target is: make the
compare-phase pass count through the 57681 basin be a function of the ORDER
class (3 classes needed for LT), and land the digit-selection counters on
1 iff a<b. `(0,0)` on the EQ image prints `00` (steps 78,654) — EQ's own
domain-0 equality is NOT correct on all pairs; only the 7 P2_STATUS vectors
are demonstrated. Evidence shas: see trace files in `~/tmp_lt/`.
12/12 remains `NOT_DEMONSTRATED`.

**Finding 42 — correction refining findings 38/41: the `stack_top` echo IS the
verdict carrier (artifact/logic dichotomy was wrong).** Re-check on the
canonical EQ image (`~/tmp_lt/eqk.mb`): `(1,2)` prints `00` @81,065 while
`(2,2)` prints `01` @81,069 — same `b`, different `a` — so the value of the
CRAZY-read cell `stack_top` (57664) at print time is NOT a pure function of
`b`: the compare-phase table cycles (the 54-vs-48 passes through 57681) write
the equality signal into 57664 and OUT echoes it. The decrement-mutation seen
in finding 38 is the computation, not an accident. Design target for LT restated
(one sentence): arrange the CMP_LT compare basin so the residue landed in the
cell read by the printing CRAZY equals 1 iff a<b (0 otherwise) over {0,1,2} —
the 8/12 a7 fork lands `b`'s residue there; shift the basin's decrement
routing so it lands the ORDER residue instead.
12/12 remains `NOT_DEMONSTRATED`.

**Finding 43 — complete MOVD cell map of the EQ verdict channel (the design
surface for LT).** From `tr_eqk_{v22,v01,v10}.txt` the compare basin is a
cycle through cells {COUNTER2_1@57121, MOVED@57122, ROT@57210/57211,
dispatch_00@57609, CRAZY@57610/57611, crazy_value MOVD@57612,
RESET_FLAG2@57687, crazy_carry MOVD@57688, add-area@57869-57874,
SUBROUTINE_FLAG1 MOVD d=57969, return group @58345-58410
(value_load_loop, value_loaded, nomorecarrykilled_2, return_from_tmp_3/4,
jmp_into_loop_from_behind, return_from_value_8/9, return_from_carry_2,
return_from_carry_was_not_set_2@58387, jump_back_to_behind@58386,
return_from_carry_was_set_2@58391, return_from_value_9@58400,
exit_inner_decrement_loop@58407, restore_...@58410), FLAG1-9 chain
@58434-58814, LOOP2@58903, LOOP2_3@58997, NO_MORE_CARRY_FLAG@58998,
CARRY@59047}. Writes to `stack_top` (57664) are IDENTICAL across vectors
(steps 11426-12120, classification phase) — they set the cell address chain,
not the digit. The digit channel is exactly one: at basin exit the final
`ROT rot A=<residue>` value (2 on `(2,2)`, 0 on `(0,1)`/`(1,0)`) flows
`PUSH_FLAG4@57874 -> COUNTER2_1@57121 -> MOVD->d stack_top_1(57659) ->
dispatch_00@57609 -> CRAZY (folds; on residue 2 it reads stack_top:1 ->
A=1) -> RESET_FLAG3@57779 -> PUSH_FLAG2@57780 -> add_pop0_done@57869 ->
PUSH_FLAG3@57870/57873 -> MOVD->d SUBROUTINE_FLAG1(57969) -> CRAZY@57610 ->
MOVED@57122 -> ret_rst_val_c1_1@58151 -> ADD_FLAG@58152 -> HALT@57082 ->
OUT`. Exit sequences are cell-for-cell identical between TRUE and FALSE
vectors; ONLY the residue value differs. LT requirement reduced to: land a
residue in the basin-exit ROT such that the CRAZY fold yields A=1 iff a<b
over {0,1,2} (residue classes needed: at least 3, currently the basin
produces {0,2}). Evidence: trace files in `~/tmp_lt/` (shas recorded at
finding 41 addendum context); generator `mbir_p2_cmp_gen.py` untouched since
`9fd3979`. 12/12 remains `NOT_DEMONSTRATED`.


**Finding 44 — decision point localized to the exit CRAZY-fold on `stack_top`
(57664) with content = residue of `a`; basin fires only in the b==2 class.**
Full 9-pair battery on `~/tmp_lt/eqk.mb` (`tr_eqk_v*.txt`): printed digit is
1 ONLY at `(2,2)` (steps 77,646; all other pairs print `00`, `(x,0)`@75,231,
`(x,1)`@75,777, `(x,2)`@77,642 — step counts depend on `b` ALONE). Refines
finding 41: the 54-vs-48 pass count through 57681 is a `b`-classifier
artifact, NOT the equality signal. The real decision window (diff of
`(0,2)` vs `(2,2)` tails): exit chain `SLOT0_FLAG@57588 -> MOVED@57210 ->
ROT (clears value->59048) -> PUSH_FLAG3@57870 -> COUNTER2_1@57121 ->
MOVD->d stack_top_1(57659) -> dispatch_00@57609 -> CRAZY@57610 FOLD`, where
the fold READS cell `stack_top`(57664) whose content at that moment equals
the residue of `a` (`stack_top:0` on `(0,2)` vs `stack_top:2` on `(2,2)`);
on nonzero content the fold chains an extra decrement round (`stack_top:2 ->
... -> stack_top:1 -> print A=1`) while zero content prints 0 directly. Fold
rounds fire only in the b==2 basin (the 54-pass class). So the observable
verdict channel is: print = (content of 57664 at exit fold) decremented once
per fold round, with basin entry gated by the b class. LT design target now
concrete: (1) gate fold entry on b > 0 (instead of b==2 only), (2) have
57664 hold a residue such that the decrement-round count yields 1 iff a<b
over {0,1,2} — e.g. land `a` and make round count = b - a clipped, or land
`2-a` and gate on b; both require changing only MOVD operands feeding the
exit fold chain (cells 57588/57609-57612/57659/57664/57870/57121), which are
address-stable targets of the classification phase, not HeLL blocks.
12/12 remains `NOT_DEMONSTRATED`.


**Finding 45 — the exit fold prints 1 IFF the CRAZY reads cell 57664 itself
(identity gate), not merely a nonzero content; SP-select table measured over
all 9 pairs.** Last labeled fold read before print on `eqk.mb`: `(x,0)` ->
`stack_top(57664):0`; `(x,1)` -> `stack_top_1(57659-60):0`; `(0,2)`,`(1,2)` ->
`stack_top_1:1` (content 1, PRINTS 0); `(2,2)` -> `stack_top:2` then
`stack_top:1` (PRINTS 1). So content-1 at the WRONG cell prints 0 while
content-1 at 57664 prints 1: the OUT-phase CRAZY's operand cell address is
itself part of the verdict channel — the dynamic stack pointer must point at
slot 0 (57664) AND its content must be the nonzero residue. Observed
EQ-image semantics reduced: print1 = [SP->57664 at exit fold] AND [content
1], and the measured SP-select over the 9 pairs is SP=57664 iff a==2&b==2 —
i.e. the image computes the CONJUNCTION of the two classes, with the classes
carried by the two push chains' MOVD d-register targets (writes observed at
steps 10841-12120, d in {57655, 57659, 57664}). LT construction problem in
final form: choose the d-register routing of the two operand-push chains and
the exit-fold round count so that [SP lands 57664] AND [content lands 1]
exactly on {(0,1),(0,2),(1,2)}. All levers are MOVD operand values in the
classification/push chains (address-stable), zero HeLL-block edits.
12/12 remains `NOT_DEMONSTRATED`.

**Finding 46 — MOVD-semantics correction: MOVD never writes memory; the slot
cell identities in findings 38/43-45 were bisect artifacts, and the true
push-delivery of `a` lands at step 60709 via the CRAZY fold.**

Instrumented re-trace of the canonical EQ image (`~/tmp_lt/eqk.mb`,
traces `~/tmp_lt/tr2_eqk_v*.txt`, v22 sha `6e33f54af27f872a...`) falsifies
the wording, not the behavior, of findings 38 and 43-45:

1. MOVD (op 40) executes `d := mem[d]` — it moves the d-register POINTER and
never writes memory. Every statement in earlier findings of the form "MOVD
wrote / pushes into stack_top" is wrong. All slot content changes come from
CRAZY (op 62) and ROT (op 39) folds executed while d happens to point at the
slot; OUT-path reads are then content-of-fold echoes (consistent with 45).

2. The tracer labels cells by greatest-label-address <= target, so the
frequent `MOVD->d stack_top_1 (d=57659)` lines are MISNAMED: 57659 is not a
label cell. True addresses from `eqk.dbg`: `stack_top_1 = 57642`,
`stack_top_1_crazy = 57641`, `stack_top_crazy = 57660`, `stack_top = 57661`.
The finding-45 gate cells (57659, 57664) are raw d-register VALUES inside the
57642..57661 span, not the labeled slot storages. The 57646/57655/57664/57673
"slots" probed in the tr2 run are jump-table cells (contents
57869/57685/57780/57682, vector-invariant) — confirming finding 45's SP-select
observation refers to d-pointer positions, which remain the correct lever.

3. Push delivery measured identical on all 9 pairs: 60700 `JMP->
return_from_stack_top_3 @ 57963`; 60706 `MOVD d := 57659` (invariant); 60709
CRAZY fold leaves `stack_top` (57661) content exactly equal to the pushed
first operand a (0 on v0x, 1 on v1x, 2 on v2x). At 11177 the same cell holds
29524 (a return pointer) — the stack-head cell alternates link-data and
operand-data; "content a" is written once and then decremented by the basin.

4. The (1,2)-vs-(2,2) paradox resolves as predicted by 45: `stack_top` shows
2->1 on BOTH (v12 at 71000/71015, v22 at 77617/77632), yet only v22 prints
0x01. A content-1 on stack_top is NOT the verdict; the verdict is the
identity of the d-position (57659 vs 57664) held at the printing CRAZY. The
tail MOVD sequences of v12/v22 are identical modulo a 4-step shift
(58391 -> 58400 -> 58410 -> 57740 -> 57606 -> HALT at 81173/81177), so the
b-dependent divergence happens strictly between 60709 and the first OUT
(~71k-77k) — the basin decay rounds, as finding 44 measured.

Consequence for the LT construction problem (45's final form unchanged,
vocabulary corrected): the levers are the d-register routing values produced
by MOVD chains and the round count of the basin folds; the design target is
that the FIRST printing CRAZY executes with d at the slot-0 position with
fold-result 1 exactly on {(0,1),(0,2),(1,2)} and never otherwise. Next
mechanistic step: re-trace v12/v22 with raw d printed at every CRAZY/ROT
write to pin the d-position of the printing fold.

12/12 remains `NOT_DEMONSTRATED`.

**Finding 47 — the verdict channel is fully pinned: printed digit = content
of `stack_top` (57661) at the printing fold; EQ image = a minus a b-gated
decrement round count; the CRAZY-writer trace confirms every slot write and
the tr3 W-lines record PRE-fold state.**

Writer-instrumented traces `~/tmp_lt/tr3_eqk_v{12,22}.txt` (sha `fdace527...`
and `2b30f6a0...`) on the canonical EQ image, plus per-vector content
correlation over the tr2 set, close the mechanism question opened by
findings 44-46:

1. Execution is BIT-IDENTICAL across vectors through step 60690; the first
   divergence is the IN reading a (v12 A=1 vs v22 A=2 at 60691). All 59k
   steps of table build/init are vector-independent (as expected).

2. Every write to the slot window 57580..57700 is a CRAZY (op 62) fold at the
   current d; W-line format records the PRE-fold state. The decrement fold is
   `crazy(59048, x) -> x-1` (observed as A=59048 lines stepping content down);
   `crazy(29524, x) -> 29524` restores the pointer word. The printed byte
   appears at OUT with A equal to the post-fold content of 57661.

3. Measured single verdict law over all 9 pairs — printed digit = the content
   of `stack_top` 57661 at the first OUT:
   preOUT content is 1 ONLY for (2,2); 0 for the other eight pairs, i.e.
   `print1 = [a - decay(b) == 1]` with decay(0)=decay(1)>=a-max, decay(2)=1.
   (2,0) and (2,1) decay to 0 before OUT (v21 shows content 1 at 72615 then a
   further decrement before OUT 79308); (2,2) decays exactly once (2->1 at
   77616-77631, OUT 1 at 77646). The tails of v12/v22 differ only by WHICH
   decay round the drain loop has completed when the OUT-table pass arrives —
   consistent with finding 44's step counts (drain rounds keyed to b) and
   45's identity gate, now without ambiguity: the "identity" that matters is
   d == 57661 at the printing fold.

4. Consequence for LT construction: the channel available for free is
   SUBTRACTIVE (a minus b-keyed round count); {(0,1),(0,2),(1,2)} is NOT a
   sublevel set of a-k(b) (0-k=1 impossible). Therefore LT needs either (i) a
   ternary CRAZY-table fold at the printing position — arrange (A, content)
   at 57661 so crazy_op lands on a 1-producing cell exactly for a<b (the
   a7 8/12 lineage does precisely this for 8 of 12 vectors) — or (ii) an
   early round-count crossover so the DECAY itself is a-keyed for one operand
   and b-keyed for the other, making preOUT content 1 on the strict-lower
   pairs. Both routes keep all levers in MOVD d-routing + fold-round counts
   over live cells; no HeLL block edits (findings 40-41 forbid those).

12/12 remains `NOT_DEMONSTRATED`.

**Finding 48 — fold-row semantics corrected and the print-time slot matrix
measured on all 9 pairs: the basin vocabulary is (i) ternary INVOLUTION
crazy(59048, .): 0->0, 1->2, 2->1 (not the decrement stated in finding 47),
(ii) class-2 CLAMP crazy(29524, .): 0->0, 1->0, 2->2, and the print-time
content matrix is rank-two: slot 57642 = [b==2] and slot 57661 =
[a==2 AND b==2].**

Writer traces tr3_eqk_v{00..22}.txt (v12 sha fdace527..., v22 sha 2b30f6a0...)
plus the oracle row probe (raw table printed here as evidence):

  crazy(29524, x) = [0,0,2,0,0,2][x]   (class-2 clamp)
  crazy(59048, x) = [0,2,1,6,8,7][x]   (ternary digit involution)
  crazy(29443, x) = [81,81,83,...]     (pointer bias)
  crazy(81, x)    = [29443,...]        (pointer bias)

Print-time content matrix (last W62 fold at each d before first OUT, window
57640..57690, all 50 cells probed per vector; only 57642 and 57661 are
a/b-varying — everything else holds pointer words 29443/29524/81/59047):

  cell 57642: 0 0 1 / 0 0 1 / 0 0 1  over (a,b) rows — equals [b==2],
              independent of a (the b-push chain classes b and nothing else)
  cell 57661: 0 0 0 / 0 0 0 / 0 0 2  — equals 2 iff a==2 AND b==2
  every other vector: print digit 0 (crazy(59048, content) with content 0/1
              gives 0/2; only content 2 gives the printed byte 1)

Corrections and consequences:

1. Finding 47's "decrement crazy(59048,x)->x-1" is WITHDRAWN: the 1<->2
   oscillations observed in v12/v22 near 70k-77k are the involution row being
   applied twice, and the true zeroing is the clamp row (A=29524), which is
   what collapsed v12's content 1 to 0 at 74459.
2. The EQ image's verdict is therefore: content_57661(print) =
   involution(clamp(involution(a))) chained by b-keyed round parity —
   an AND of the two class-2 signals. Rank of the observable channel set is
   exactly 2 (b-class at 57642, a-AND-b-class at 57661); no cell in the
   window stores any other a/b-dependent residue at print time.
3. LT target {(0,1),(0,2),(1,2)} is NOT realizable from the existing
   single-fold print channel on these two cells alone (neither [b==2] nor
   [a==2 AND b==2] equals a<b, and one fold = one cell). The a7 8/12 design
   must have routed a THIRD channel (or changed the print fold's cell
   identity per phase); the immediate next measurement is the identical
   print-time matrix for the a7 LT lineage image (frozen build a7base.mb) to
   see which cells it made a/b-varying and where its remaining 4-vector
   misses come from.

12/12 remains `NOT_DEMONSTRATED`.

**Finding 49 — CRAZY is DIGIT-WISE over 10 ternary digits with a 3-map fold
alphabet; the single-trit fold monoid is 17 elements and CANNOT realize the
LT row for b=1; therefore any 12/12 design must use multi-trit encoding, and
the a7 lineage's remaining failures are localized to b=1 being information-
theoretically invisible at print time.**

Oracle source read (`tools/oracle_classic.py crazy_op`): result digit k =
`_CRAZY[content_trit_k][A_trit_k]`, table `_CRAZY = ((1,0,0),(1,0,2),(2,2,1))`
indexed [y][x]. Consequences measured:

1. For a fixed A-trit x, the fold is a map on the content trit:
   P0: y->(1,1,2) (bump-zero), P1: y->(0,0,2) (class-2 clamp),
   P2: y->(0,2,1) (involution). These are the ONLY ternary-preserving folds;
   the earlier finding-48 rows C/I/F are exactly P1/P2/P0 relabeled.
2. The composition monoid of {P0,P1,P2} has 17 elements; enumerated exhaustively.
   The LT verdict rows as unary maps on a (digit printed = 1 iff a<b) are
   g_0=(0,0,0) REACHABLE (P1∘P2∘P1), g_2=(1,1,0) REACHABLE (P2∘P1∘P2∘P0),
   but g_1=(1,0,0) is NOT in the monoid. Proof: no composition of P0,P1,P2
   maps 0->1 while mapping 1->0. Hence a one-trit channel cascade with
   b-keyed fold selection cannot compute LT — structural, not an artifact of
   the current image.
3. Print-time matrices settle the a7 channel inventory: a7 (tr3_a7_v*.txt):
   cell 57661 = a EXACTLY for all nine pairs; cell 57642 = [b==2]; every other
   window cell pointer-invariant; (0,0) and (0,1) print-time states are
   IDENTICAL — b=1 leaves no ternary-visible residue anywhere in
   57560..57760 at print time. a7's observed behavior (print 2 on b==2,
   0 otherwise) is fully explained: the printing fold reads 57642 with P2
   (content 1 -> A 2 -> OUT byte 0x02).
4. EQ image contrast (finding 48): same two channels but the printing fold
   reads 57661 — the images differ only in the d-identity of the printing
   fold; the a/b routing is the shared skeleton. This unifies the two
   lineages: EQ = fold-on-slot0([a==2 AND b==2]); a7 = fold-on-slot1([b==2]).
5. Design requirement sharpened for 12/12: the b-channel must become
   multi-trit (b=1 distinguishable) by print time. Routes: (i) exploit the
   digit-wise freedom — encode (a,b) in the trit positions of one counter
   cell (e.g. the 57614 value cell seen cycling 1053/39366-class numbers) so
   each fold applies a DIFFERENT P per position; printed byte = result mod 256,
   a nonlinear cross-position read; (ii) lengthen the b=1 basin by exactly one
   fold round so slot1 content 57642 reaches 2 for b==1 instead of 0 (P1∘P2
   on raw b gives (0,2,0): b=1->2 while b=2->0 — an ANTI-signal usable with
   involution parity). Route (ii) is a pure round-count edit in the b-chain
   and is the cheapest experiment; it converts the channel from [b==2] to
   [b==1], and combined with the a-signal at slot 0 gives channels
   {a, [b==1]} — still insufficient alone, but stacking one more b round
   class distinguishes all three b values across two cells:
   57642: [b==2]-style parity, 57661: a. A full feasibility sweep over
   (round-count edits) x (fold-cell identities) is the next computational
   step; the monoid above bounds the search.

12/12 remains `NOT_DEMONSTRATED`.

**Finding 50 — round-parity edits are insufficient for LT (measured
exhaustive-negative): all vectors run exactly TWO P2 folds on slot 1 before
OUT; b=0 and b=1 are bit-identical at print time (raw b is clamped to 0 by
the push chain before drain), and neither the per-b-keyed fold monoid over
slot-parity nor the per-a-keyed variant contains the required rows.**

Extension of finding 49 with the dispatch mechanism and a bounded exhaustive
search (`~/tmp_lt/` brute-forcing the two-cell fold algebra):

1. Print-dispatch mechanism (a7, tr3_a7_v02): MOVD `d := 57640` (value cell),
   the following JMP executes with d auto-incremented to 57641 and reads
   `mem[57641] = 57609` (dispatch_00 CRAZY cell); at the fold step d has
   incremented again to 57642 — so slot 1 is folded by CELL IDENTITY via the
   d-register auto-increment race, exactly the structure EQ uses one cell
   earlier. Fold rounds measured: 2 for every class; pre-fold contents
   (2,1)->(0,0); b=2 -> (2,1); output digit = second-fold result (b=2 -> 2,
   else 0). The drain-entry content of slot 1 is clamp(b) — raw b=1 has
   already been clamped to 0 by the push chain, hence invisibility.

2. Exhaustive negative results (algebra: cells {slot0=a-content, slot1=b-
   clamp}, fold maps restricted to the ternary-preserving P0/P1/P2 plus
   pointer-A rows; print = A % 256): (i) no shared fold sequence of length
   <= 8 from state (a,b) realizes LT; (ii) allowing per-b-class sequences of
   length <= 6 after a shared prefix <= 2 over all A0 inits {59048, 29524, 0}
   still zero hits — the required rows are h_1 = (1,0,0) [not in the
   17-element monoid] under any b-keyed round selection; (iii) ROT-output
   values (19683k+) never reduce mod 256 to 1 or 0 with the small-digit
   structure. Conclusion: within the current two-slot geometry, NO
   size-neutral table edit of fold-round parity can reach 12/12 — the
   missing capacity must come from the MULTI-TRIT counter chain (cell 57614
   orbits 1053->351->117->39->13 observed before OUT in both lineages),
   whose mod-256 readout is a nonlinear decoder outside the ternary algebra
   searched above.

3. Strategy update for task P2: reverse-engineer the counter chain of the
   a7 8/12 image — which fold steps read/write 57614, how its trit pattern
   encodes the partial LT verdict, and where the four failing vectors
   ((0,1),(0,2),(1,2),(2,2) at print bytes) diverge from the target — then
   propose table-cell relabel edits that shift the counter orbit for the
   missing classes only.

12/12 remains `NOT_DEMONSTRATED`.

# Finding 51 (2026-10-13, session 5d) — the single b=1 information-loss site is localized: cell 57537 classifier fold reads a non-injective CRAZY row

Lineage under measurement: a7 LT image `a7base.mb` (sha256 `b73ccd25275f6292…`, commit
`a7a040e` generator output, 8/12). All 9 cmp vectors, python oracle semantics, step-exact.

## 1. Where raw b enters the decision, and what it lands on

Verified writer trace (`~/tmp_lt/p57/v*.txt`, probe `probe57.py`, exact oracle semantics
incl. post-execution cell translation and d auto-increment):

    69853  W62 d=57537  pre=29525  A_in=b  ->  post = 29525 (b=0, b=1) | 29524 (b=2)

Cell 57537 is the third cell of the NOP pointer table (static alternating 29443/81 across
57528-57545; 57537 static = 81). The fold target is reached by `MOVD d := 57535` at step
69851 plus two d auto-increments (57536, 57537). Six cells hold the pointer value 57537's
source 57535 at that moment: 57581, 57819, 57878, 58140, 58148, 58165 (`scan51.py`).

57537's whole write history is VECTOR-INDEPENDENT up to 69853 — identical for all 9 vectors
(probe dumps `p57/`):

    1920   81 -> 29524          (A=0)
    57327  29524 -> 29523        (A=1)
    61186/61204/61225/61281: 29523 -> 0 -> 0 -> 29524 -> 29523   (A = 29524, 59048, 0, 1)
    65138/65156/65177/65233: 29523 -> 0 -> 0 -> 29524 -> 29525   (A = 29524, 59048, 0, 2)
    69853  29525 -> crazy(b, 29525)                              <-- classifier

So the content presented to the classifier is fixed at 29525 = trits [2,1,1,1,1,1,1,1,1,1].

## 2. Why that content is fatal — row table

`_CRAZY[y][x]`, rows keyed by content trit y, columns by A trit x:

    y=0 -> (1,0,0)   collapses {1,2}
    y=1 -> (1,0,2)   BIJECTION (injective in x)
    y=2 -> (2,2,1)   collapses {0,1}

Trit 0 of 57537 at 69853 is y=2, hence b=0 and b=1 both map to 29525 and b=2 to 29524.
This is the ONLY place raw b is consumed for the verdict, and it is the complete
explanation of the a7 failure set: b=1 is bit-identical to b=0 from step 69853 onward
(measured earlier: v00/v01 print states identical). Corollary of the same law: had the
content been 29524 (y=1 trit 0), the fold would have produced 29524 / 29523 / 29525 for
b = 0 / 1 / 2 — a full 3-class readout. The bump law on an all-1s cell is
`crazy(x, 29524) = bijection of x's trits`, and the image already manufactures 29524 from
`crazy(0, 0)` (steps 61225, 65177) — the bijective constant is producible in-image.

## 3. The bump that destroys injectivity comes from the harness tail byte

57537 is pushed from 29524 to 29525 at step 65233 by `A = 2`, and that 2 is INPUT: the run
executes exactly 7 IN instructions, consuming the harness framing
`01 {a} 01 {b} 02 10 00` in order at steps 57321, 61048, 61275, 65001, 65227, ~727xx/728xx,
~757xx/759xx (`grep -E "^ *[0-9]+ +IN +IN" tr3_a7_v*.txt`). So the fixed tail constants
0x02 / 0x10 / 0x00 are program-visible control values: they bump 57537 at 65233 / 72890 /
75974 (16 -> 29517, 0 -> 29524). The classifier row is therefore chosen by the *read
schedule*, not by an immutable property of the image.

## 4. Second bottleneck (must be fixed together)

Even with 57537 bijective, the next fold writes the counter cell 57614 with content
59048 = trits all-2 (row y=2, `crazy(x, 59048)` maps x-trit 0 and 1 both to 2, only 2 -> 1).
Verified: at 69866 A_in is 29525 (b<2) vs 29524 (b=2) -> post 59047 vs 59048, which is the
orbit divergence localized in finding 50 at counter index 211 / step 69909. So a 3-class
classifier feeding an all-2 counter re-collapses {0,1} at one step. Any LT design needs
BOTH the classifier cell and the counter seed cell on all-1s (29524-type) content at their
respective folds, i.e. two row-selection edits, not one.

## 5. Consequences for the design route

- The a7 lineage's 4 failures are not a distribution of bad luck across the pipeline; they
  are one row selection (content trit 0 = 2 at step 69853) plus its echo at 57614.
- Levers, in ascending blast radius: (i) change which byte the 65233 bump consumes
  (read-schedule edit; A=0 there leaves 57537 = 29524 -> injective), (ii) change the
  pointer cell that supplies d=57535 at 69851 so the classifier folds a different cell
  (12 table cells hold 29524 at that instant: 57595, 57719, 57904, 57937, 57979, 58003,
  58073, 58089, 58112, 58136, 58257, 58351), (iii) rebuild with the counter seed also on
  29524. Each of (i)/(ii) perturbs the shared 4-write cycle used by all later rounds, so
  they must be scored on all 9 vectors + 3 baselines, not reasoned about locally.
- Still NOT_DEMONSTRATED: any 12/12 image. No candidate built in this step; HEAD remains
  the EQ probe lineage.

Artifacts (sha256, `~/tmp_lt/`):

    8d8a515439bd046c573a1e51990641b02ac3c38664b59fdf64cc94d90774ff2f  probe57.py
    7a81f31c8bc3105b5adfe8124926510f7c80e70a5aa45a9e61d105efe4ba3599  scan51.py
    b73ccd25275f6292f74e2d4843e32e24dfe6191e00d0504868c60449937dfd63  a7base.mb
    d21bd14d60e3cc1cd5bb2335defe485b76bf74788cf87c58373c359a9da9cec9  a7base.dbg
    ef727acea6a8e0f3aa6321c457a912a7c21eef5d27f8b7f58a64bb151658cbf5  p57/v00.txt (= v10 = v20)
    564d7e0bdf4a8bca074c4b567d1c5295dc54a173cd2111652d054f21e4abb973  p57/v01.txt (= v11)
    fa86fd76b9ccc3c3d3b50f00de074036506d498133787f71c8fe4e05428f0c69  p57/v02.txt (= v12 = v22)
    fc244819a0fa00049fc81e43491a3c1739da3ef9fb37bb799c36edcf8d8d5255  tr3_a7_v00.txt
    136b6074a63ef36a59e4e63825537b154d2b489e65a828d78ac0021d3788c848  tr3_a7_v01.txt
    ebcc62dfe43c23fb465c406895e1f5d49484dd1b2ac98b7c834ffff487d40a8a  tr3_a7_v02.txt

The dump diff is the whole result in one line: `p57/v00` vs `p57/v01` differ in exactly one
field — the classifier's input (A_in=0 vs A_in=1) — while its post-state is byte-identical
(29525); `p57/v00` vs `p57/v10` are byte-identical (a never touches this cell). The 9 dumps
collapse into 3 classes keyed by `b<2 | b=2`, i.e. 57537 carries at most 2 b-states.

# Finding 52 — the a7 lineage has NO combination site: a and b never meet in any fold
`[CURRENT]` measured 2026-10-13, image `a7base.mb` (HEAD lineage of the a7a040e patch), python
step-exact probe VM validated bit-identical to `tools/oracle_classic.run`.

## 1. Instrument: probe VM, and what the four candidate edits actually score

The probe VM replays `oracle_classic.run` step-for-step (incl. the post-execution `_ENC`
translation of `mem[c]` and the unconditional `c`/`d` increments) and allows a runtime hook to
rewrite a fold's target before any expensive `lmao` rebuild. Control, all 12 vectors:

    cmp_lt(0,0)  want=00   sim=00     oracle=00     steps=79167/79167 identical=True
    cmp_lt(0,2)  want=01   sim=02     oracle=02     steps=78974/78974 identical=True
    baseline1    want=41   sim=41     oracle=41     steps=67563/67563 identical=True
    baseline2    want=4241 sim=4241   oracle=4241   steps=74600/74600 identical=True
    -> 12/12 identical=True on every vector (full log: f52_evidence.txt)

Scoring the three levers left open by finding 51, plus the finding-50 hope, on
9 cmp_lt vectors + 3 baselines:

| edit | score | behaviour |
|---|---|---|
| redirect classifier folds 57537 -> 57536 (lever ii) | **1/12** | every vector HALTs at step **57404**; cell 57536 is live table data, so the "one-cell pointer edit" is not local at all |
| force classifier content to bijective 29524 (lever i) | **8/12** | identical verdict *set* to HEAD — the b=1 loss moves downstream to the counter fold on all-2s content |
| force counter folds (57614) to bijective 29524 (lever iii) | **7/12** | print becomes an **exact carrier of b**: output 00/01/02 for b=0/1/2 at uniform 71360 steps, all 3 baselines intact — and completely independent of a |
| inject seed value V into 57614 (finding-50 "free decoder") | no V yields 01 | the printed digit is a whole-trajectory property, not a locally decodable field |

## 2. Census: 909 aligned fold sites, zero of them see both inputs

`fold_census2.py` records every ROT/CRAZY write after step 60000 per target cell, aligned **from
the end** (b=2 vectors run 11 folds fewer than b!=2 — 909 vs 920 — so forward alignment is
invalid; the tail is where the print channel lives). Over all 909 aligned sites:

    CROSS sites (one input carries a, the other carries b): 0

    per-cell dependency map (cA/cB = content varies with a/b, aA/aB = A_in varies with a/b):
      d=57537: sites=17  cB=3  aB=3
      d=57563: sites=111 cB=37 aB=36
      d=57595: sites=4   cB=3  aB=3
      d=57614: sites=437 cB=261 aB=260
      d=57642: sites=9   cB=8  aB=8
      d=57661: sites=2   cA=1 aA=2
      d=57690: sites=63  cB=10 aB=10
      d=57719: sites=2   cA=1 aA=2

`fold_census3.py` repeats this from step 1 (18807 folds/vector) and the picture is the same, only
sharper: **six cells carry b, two cells carry a, and the two sets are disjoint.** The last
a-dependent fold anywhere in the machine is step **71178**; b-dependent folds continue to step
**78965**, i.e. into the print channel.

## 3. Mechanism of the a-loss (`window_trace.py`, steps 71169/71178, corrected A_in)

    step 71169 c=57610 CRAZY d=57719 pre=29524 post=29524 A_in=0      (a=0)
    step 71169 c=57610 CRAZY d=57719 pre=29523 post=29524 A_in=0      (a>=1)
    step 71178 c=57610 CRAZY d=57661 pre=0     post=0     A_in=29524  (a=0)
    step 71178 c=57610 CRAZY d=57661 pre=1     post=0     A_in=29524  (a>=1)

`a` survives the classifier *only* as the low trit of two cell contents (29524 vs 29523, and 0 vs
1) — and in both cases the fold that reads them maps the two a-states to the **same** post value.
The a-signal is not lost downstream: it is crushed by the very fold addressed to it, twice,
because the A register feeding both folds is a **constant** (0 and 29524). `w2_00.txt` vs
`w2_01.txt` (b varies, a=0) differ in **zero** lines in this window: b never reaches here.

`combine_check.py` exhausts the whole machine over all 59049 A values:

    A values distinguishing (29524,29523): 19683/59049
    A values distinguishing (0,1)        : 19683/59049
      trit0 of distinguishing A = [2]; set == {A : trit0(A)=2}? True (19683 vs 19683)

So the exact law is: **a CRAZY fold preserves the a-carrier iff trit0(A) = 2** (A mod 3 == 2).
HEAD feeds it A = 0 (trit0 0) and A = 29524 (trit0 1) — it misses the condition in both folds, by
different residues. A correct image must route a **b-dependent A with trit0 = 2** into an
**a-carrying content cell**; that fold is then by construction a function of (a, b).

## 4. The a7 classifier is provably not enough — 2-way a-discrimination cannot be routed around

The surviving a-carrier distinguishes {a=0} from {a>=1} (nA=2 of 3 pairs; the a=1 vs a=2 pair is
already merged). But the required verdicts on that merged class are contradictory:

    (a=1, b=2) want 01   |   (a=2, b=2) want 00

same a-class, same b, opposite verdict. No read-schedule, pointer or seed edit that preserves the
2-way classifier can therefore reach 12/12 — this closes lever-by-lever what finding 51 left as
"levers in ascending blast radius". The a7 patch lineage is **structurally finished**, not merely
unlucky.

## 5. Spec derived for the next design (three independent requirements)

1. a **3-way** a-discriminator (the a7 classifier's {1,2} merge is fatal, §4);
2. a **combination fold**: a-carrying content cell x b-carrying A with trit0(A)=2 (§3 law);
3. a **bijective carrier** into print: content all-1s (29524), not all-2s (59048), on both the
   classifier and the counter (finding 51 row law + §1 counter result, which already shows a
   29524-seeded print path delivers b losslessly).

Still NOT_DEMONSTRATED: any 12/12 image. No candidate built in this step; HEAD remains the EQ
probe lineage.

    d7c1d0c62948f5513cbfdb09c78830160a9304bcdde7248d836239b8c775d077  f52_run.py

    6f1bb42dc0bcae20e32cb4aed6d01143116c1f6c1353e49aa5a022ec77dc27ff  f52_combine.txt

# Finding 53 — the collapse→bijection law is PREDICTIVE: one HELL constant edit gives 9/12 on both engines
`[CURRENT]` measured 2026-10-13, HELL source of the a7a040e lineage, rebuilt with the in-repo
`third_party/lmao/lmao` (v0.6.0), scored with `tools/oracle_classic.py` **and**
`runners/malbolge-original/malbolge`.

## 1. The toolchain is reproducible (this was the missing precondition)

`lmao -d -o a7cand.mb a7cand.hell` on `git show a7a040e:vm_malbolge/src/mbir_cmp_lt.hell`
regenerates an image behaviourally identical to `a7base.mb`: same 57,517 bytes, 82 byte-level
differences confined to the trailing offset 57416+ region (the CRC/encoding tail), identical
verdicts. So finding 51's three "levers" can now be tested as **real image edits in ~5 s**
(0.3 s build + 12 vectors), instead of being argued about. That changes the epistemics of this
milestone: from now on every claim about a lever is a measurement.

## 2. Runtime cells now have HELL names (`a7cand.dbg` `:SOURCEPOSITIONS:`)

| runtime cell | label | HELL line | role |
|---|---|---|---|
| 57537 | `value_C1` | 193 (`C1`) | classifier fold target (finding 51) |
| 57563 | `tmp` | — | b-carrier |
| 57614 | `value` | 179 (`C1`) | counter / scratch |
| 57719 | **`stack_scratch`** | 241 (`C1`) | **slot-0 data = where `a` lives** |
| 57661 | **`stack_top`** | 271 (`C1`) | **slot-0 pointer = second `a` carrier** |

The constant cells that feed those folds: cell 57904 ← HELL **466** `ROT C1 R_ROT` (A = 29524),
cell 57913 ← HELL **472** `ROT 0 R_ROT` (A = 0). These two lines are the `diff_is_2` /
`add_rst0_done` pair of the LT verdict encoder (lines 464-482: 466 reset, 472/477 push-0,
481 push-1). Finding 52 §3 said the a-carrier is crushed because the fold receives a *constant*
A; the debugger now names the exact statements that choose that constant.

## 3. Prediction from the law, confirmed on both engines

Finding 52 law: a fold preserves a carrier iff **trit0(A) = 2**; content C2 (all-2s, 59048) is a
collapsing row, C1 (all-1s, 29524) is the bijective row. HELL line 384 (`add_val_clean` — the
slot-1/b pop: `ROT C2 R_ROT; R_PUSH_FLAG3; MOVED stack_top_1_crazy`) therefore sits a collapsing
constant in front of a fold that carries b. Swapping exactly that token, `384: C2 -> C1`, and
rebuilding gives **`a7c9`**:

    cmp_lt(0,0) PASS   cmp_lt(0,1) FAIL(00)  cmp_lt(0,2) FAIL(00)  cmp_lt(1,0) PASS
    cmp_lt(1,1) PASS   cmp_lt(1,2) FAIL(00)  cmp_lt(2,0) PASS      cmp_lt(2,1) PASS
    cmp_lt(2,2) PASS   baseline1 41 PASS     baseline2 4241 PASS   baseline3 empty PASS
    == 9/12, oracle == runner on every vector, all 3 baselines intact

This is the first *gained* vector in this milestone since the lineage forked: (2,2), which HEAD
wrongly printed as 02, is now correct. The remaining 3 failures are exactly the 3 true pairs, all
printing 00 — i.e. the machine now has **all six false verdicts right** and is missing only the
positive direction, which finding 52 proved needs a combination fold (a-carrying content x
b-carrying A with trit0(A)=2) that this architecture never schedules: the verdict encoder loads A
from constants (lines 466/472/477/481) immediately before the slot-0 folds, so no b-derived value
is ever in the register at that moment.

## 4. Single-site exhaustive ROT-constant sweep (33 sites x 4 alternatives = 129 rebuilds)

Every `ROT <const> R_ROT` statement in the a7 HELL was replaced by each of the other four
constants, rebuilt and scored (log `f53_sweep.txt`, per-site table `f53_sites_summary.md`).
Baseline a7 = 8/12. Score distribution over the 129 candidates:

| score | # candidates | meaning |
|---|---|---|
| 8/12 | 77 | exact no-op (identical failset 01,02,12,22) |
| **9/12** | **2** | the only gains: 384 `C2->C1` (fails 01,02,12 — all baselines intact) and 560 `C2->C1` (fails 01,22,**bl2**) |
| 6/12 | 8 | 502, 508 (slot-1 pointer folds) |
| 3/12 | 6 | 384's other 3 alternatives |
| 2/12 | 15 | 560/566/621/639/645/673 non-C1 alternatives |
| 1/12 | 17 | arithmetic core and encoder-mutating choices |
| 0/12 | 4 | 748 `C1->C2`, 755 `C2->C0/0/3` |

Three structural facts fall out of the table, all consistent with finding 52 and none of them
arguable any more because they are measured:

1. **The LT verdict encoder is inert.** HELL lines 466, 472, 477, 481 (the `diff_is_2` /
   `add_rst0_done` / push-0 / push-1 block that finding 52 named as the decoder) give 8/12 with
   the *identical* failset under **all 15** alternatives. Changing the constant that feeds the
   slot-0 folds changes nothing, which is exactly what "that fold's content never carries `b`"
   predicts. The encoder is not the bottleneck; the missing combination fold is.
2. **`C2 -> C1` is the only productive token swap**, and it works at precisely the two folds whose
   content is a live `b` carrier (384 `add_val_clean` = slot-1 pop, 560 `pop_slot_1`). Replacing a
   collapsing row by the bijective row is worth exactly one vector each.
3. **The arithmetic core is a cliff.** 748/755 (increment/decrement carry rotation) drop to 0-1/12
   for every alternative, so the bijection heuristic is scoped to data folds — it must not be
   applied to the carry chain.

Only 384's 9/12 preserves the three baselines, so **`a7c9` is the canonical new best**; 560's 9/12
is not a milestone candidate (it prints `1b41` where `bl2` must be `4241`).

## 5. Hill-climb: both 9/12 bases are hard plateaus

Re-running the full 33x4 sweep from each 9/12 image (`f53_h9.txt` from `a7c9.hell`, `f53_h560.txt`
from `a7c560.hell`), i.e. every *pair* containing a known-good edit:

| base | candidates | keep base score | strictly better | max |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `a7c9` (384 C2->C1) | 132 | 79 | **0** | 9/12 |
| `a7c560` (560 C2->C1) | 131 | 78 | **0** | 9/12 |

Combined total in this step: **392 rebuilt-and-scored images, none above 9/12**; the two good edits
are mutually exclusive (applying 560 on top of 384 regresses to 8/12). Within the
ROT-constant-edit class the reachable set is therefore {8, 9} and 9 is maximal: the remaining three
vectors (01, 02, 12 — the true pairs) cannot be obtained by choosing a different A constant,
because finding 52 proved no A constant carries `b` and this sweep now proves no A constant is even
*read* by the encoder.

## 6. Consequence for the milestone

`9/12` dual-engine is the new measured best and it is **not** 12/12: milestone P2 stays
`NOT_DEMONSTRATED`. The edit class that can close it is now pinned by two independent negatives:
a fold must receive a **`b`-dependent `a` register**, which in Classic is produced only by ROT
(`oracle_classic.py:76-79`: `mem[d] = rotate(mem[d]); a = mem[d]`) or by a previous CRAZY. That
means redirecting which **cell** the ROT at the verdict-encoder steps points at (a `MOVED`/pointer
edit in the HeLL), not which constant it names. Next step (finding 54): sweep the ROT-preceding
`MOVED <label>` targets at the encoder/decoder steps over the live carrier cells.

# Finding 54 — dated correction to finding 53's row/column vocabulary, plus the HEAD lineage measured (identical plateau)
`[CURRENT]` measured 2026-10-13. This finding **corrects prose** of finding 53 and adds the HEAD
lineage sweep. No measurement of finding 53 is retracted: the image `lt_a7c9_probe.mb` still scores
9/12 with `oracle_output == runner_output` on all 12 vectors and all three baselines intact.

## 1. Correction: which constant preserves what (finding 53 §3-§4 and the probe JSON `note` are inverted)

The oracle folds `mem[d] = crazy_op(a, mem[d])` (`tools/oracle_classic.py:82-84`) and the table is
indexed `_CRAZY[content_trit][A_trit]` with `_CRAZY = ((1,0,0),(1,0,2),(2,2,1))`
(`oracle_classic.py:28-36`). Reading the rows correctly, there are **two orthogonal injectivity laws**,
and finding 53 conflated them:

| law | condition | verified |
|---|---|---|
| **column law — the fold preserves CONTENT** | `trit0(A) == 2`, i.e. `A % 3 == 2` | `content_trit -> result_trit0` is `[1,1,2]` for trit0(A)=0, `[0,0,2]` for 1, `[0,2,1]` (a permutation) for 2 |
| **row law — the fold preserves the A value** | content = all-1s = 29524 | row 1 = `(1,0,2)` is a permutation in `A_trit`; rows 0 and 2 are not |

ROT leaves the three carriers fixed (`rotate(0)=0`, `rotate(29524)=29524`, `rotate(59048)=59048`), and
`C2 = 59048 = 2222222222_3` has **trit0 = 2**, while `C1 = 29524 = 1111111111_3` has trit0 = 1.

So finding 53's productive edit `C2 -> C1` at `add_val_clean` does **not** "replace a collapsing row by
the bijective row"; it does the opposite: it **destroys** the distinction that the preserving constant
C2 was carrying through that fold. The (2,2) false positive disappears precisely because the fold stops
publishing the `b == 2` class into the slot-1 cell. The same wording appears in
`vm_malbolge/evidence/lt_a7c9_probe_harness.json` → `note`; that file is committed evidence of the run
and is **left byte-identical**, and this section is the correction. The law itself (finding 52
"trit0(A)==2") was right; only the labels attached to `C1`/`C2` were swapped.

Lesson recorded for the design: **maximising bijectivity is the wrong heuristic here.** The verdict
encoder's job is to *collapse* to two byte classes (00/01), so edits are judged by which class map they
induce, not by information preservation.

## 2. HEAD and the historical a7a040e lineage are behaviourally identical

`vm_malbolge/src/mbir_cmp_lt.hell` at HEAD is sha `9a30a700…` (822 lines) and differs from
`git show a7a040e:…` (817 lines) in every line, yet:

```
headcand.mb (rebuilt from HEAD by lmao)   8/12  fails=01,02,12,22
mbir_cmp_lt.mb (committed HEAD image)     8/12  fails=01,02,12,22
a7cand.mb    (rebuilt from a7a040e)       8/12  fails=01,02,12,22
```

and `headcand.mb` differs from the committed image only past offset ~57058 (the CRC/encoding tail) —
the reproducibility result of finding 53 §1 holds for HEAD too, so **HEAD is also just the EQ probe**
(plus the (2,2) artifact). Consequence: sweeping HEAD instead of a7 is not a second chance at the same
edit class; it is the same classifier reached by two source texts.

## 3. HEAD single-site ROT sweep: 34 sites, 136 rebuilds, max 9/12

`f54_head_sweep.txt`, distribution `0:7 1:24 2:7 3:9 5:5 6:17 7:11 8:49 9:7`, `12/12 candidates: []`.
Only three sites are productive and they all move the score from 8 to 9 by removing the (2,2) false
positive (failset becomes exactly the three TRUE pairs):

| HELL line | statement | alternatives reaching 9/12 |
|---|---|---|
| 386 `add_val_clean` | `ROT C2 R_ROT` (slot-1 pop fold) | `->C1` only (others 6/12) |
| 427 `add_val_clean_op1` | `ROT C2 R_ROT` (slot-0 pop fold) | **all four** (`C1, C0, 0, 3`) |
| 486 `add_pop0_done` | `ROT 3 R_ROT` (verdict value) | `->0`, `->C0` (others stay 8/12) |

Line 427 is the strongest structural datum of the whole milestone: *any* non-C2 constant there gives
9/12, i.e. it is not the value that matters but the **loss of the C2-preserving fold**.

## 4. Every combination of the productive sites converges to one machine

Measured with `rot_edit.py` on HEAD (5 runs): `427=C1&486=0`, `386=C1&486=0`, `427=C1&386=C1`,
`427=C1&386=C1&486=0`, `486=0&427=C0` — **all five give the identical verdict table**

    00=00 01=00 02=00 10=00 11=00 12=00 20=00 21=00 22=00  bl1=41 bl2=4241 bl3=(empty)   == 9/12

i.e. the machine is then the **constant-00 kernel** on the whole LT domain. The `01` verdict is not
"mis-routed for three pairs"; after the false positive is removed it is **unreachable**. Trace of the
still-broken HEAD (`window_trace.py`, steps 78000-81300): `a=2,b=2` executes 1505 more events than
`a=2,b=1` (2542 vs 1037 window lines, 81177 vs 79308 total steps) — the extra ~1.5k events are the
counter-orbit chain that produces the byte `01`, so the 01 route exists and fires only on that one
class. **Therefore 12/12 requires making that route fire on {(0,1),(0,2),(1,2)} and stay silent
elsewhere: a branch-gating problem, not a constant problem.**

## 5. New edit class opened: branch rewiring (in progress)

`branch_sweep.py` rewrites the **destinations** inside the handler window (370-520): every
`MOVED <label>` and every conditional `<FLAG> <label>` whose target is one of the 22 labels defined in
that window (`diff_is_0/1/2`, `op2_nonzero`, `op2_is_2`, `load_diff_0`, `check_diff_1_or_0`,
`add_push_common`, …). 38 branch sites x 21 targets = **798 rebuilt candidates**
(`f54_branch_head.txt`), scored with the oracle. First results (through site 5) already show rewirings
at 9/12 (`384 OP1_FLAG add_val_clean_op1 -> dec_op1 / diff_is_2_from_phase2 / return_from_dec_op1_1 /
return_from_dec_op1_2`, `388 MOVED stack_top_1_crazy -> add_val_clean_op1`), none above 9 yet. The
completed table and any 10+/12 candidate are recorded as finding 55.

Status: **12/12 remains `NOT_DEMONSTRATED`.** New measured best stays 9/12 (dual-engine, baselines
intact); the HEAD lineage is swept and is the same plateau; the productive direction is now pinned to
the verdict-branch gate rather than to constants.
